<section class="block block-block clearfix" id="block-block-6">
<h2 class="block-title">What is RiffTrax?</h2>
<p>Mike Nelson, Bill Corbett and Kevin Murphy, the former stars of <a href="/mst3k">Mystery Science Theater 3000</a>, create commentaries for B-movie oddities and Hollywood blockbusters. It's like watching a movie with your funniest friends!</p>
<p><a href="/aboutus"><img class="img-responsive b-lazy b-loaded" src="https://www.rifftrax.com/sites/default/files/whatIsRT-thumb.jpg"/></a></p>
</section>
